# Inn Trouble - ASSEMBLED - FORMATTED_Greek — PROOFREAD (2026-10-27)

Full bilingual read, chapter by chapter, of `translated\Inn Trouble - ASSEMBLED - FORMATTED_Greek.docx` (1776 paragraphs) against `translate_now\Inn Trouble - ASSEMBLED - FORMATTED.docx`. Series sources: corrected Flour Play Greek (Book 3) and its ledgers/decisions (FP-EL-1…12); Frost and Found Greek (Book 1, raw). Applied fixes: see `_APPLIED.md` (965 entries, 0 refused). Decisions: `PROOFREAD_DECISIONS2.md` IT-EL-1…12.

Prior report: none existed for this edition (0/0 findings to re-verify).

## Summary of defects
- **Clue chain (worst).** The confrontation [1377] left the clue in English inside italics: «περιγράφουν την καρφίτσα ως _the sapphire at its heart_». The Ch22 recap [1231] had «Το ζαφείρι στην καρδιά **του**» (masculine) against «…στην καρδιά **της**» everywhere else. Now identical at all seven places (note [149], Jessica's reading [151], [185], Theo's card [1103], [1105], [1107], Ch22 [1231], Ch23 [1269], Ch26 [1377]) — five Greek words, so "the same five words" holds.
- **Note vs letters.** The note is «σημείωμα» throughout; the 1924 letters «γράμματα». Three breaks fixed: Ch19 [1113] «επέτρεψε σε κάποιον άλλον να **τα** γράψει» (grammatically "to write the letters") → «να γράψει τα σημειώματα»; Ch23 [1257] [1269] switched the Blackburn letters to «επιστολές»; Ch20 [1168] [1194] Fiona's "my letters" (= her mail) used «γράμματα» → «αλληλογραφία». Ch19/22/26 deduction checked against the English.
- **Deed** rendered two ways (συμβόλαιο / τίτλος ιδιοκτησίας); unified to «συμβόλαιο» with agreement (Ch16, Ch23, Ch24); Ch23 [1281] "the green's deed" had become the inn's.
- **Dialogue system (ruling 1):** 26 of 31 chapters were in em-dash turns (often with reopening dashes, sometimes dashes before narration); 262 paragraphs rebuilt as «…», είπε. with the attribution restored from the English — the convention of Flour Play Greek (comma/full stop outside the closing guillemet, FP-EL-8).
- **Machine-translator note printed in the prose** (Ch24 [1286]): «σκληrυμένοι [note: keeping draft spelling or fixing to σκληρυμένοι; let's fix to σκληρυμένοι]» — deleted.
- **Leaked alphabets/words:** Cyrillic «σωριάζваясь» [45]; Vietnamese «từ» ×3 ([133] [1099] [1581] …); Hebrew/Arabic/Armenian letters inside words («καρφιצωμένη» [157], «Σام» [527], «διάπارτα» [978], «Αقرά» [1023], «γανטωμένο» [1221], «γανտωμένο» [1472]); Latin fragments inside Greek words ×20+ («καμπulωτή», «πιingouen», «Σχολieα», «έριχarla», «σιδη Dera», «χρωstούσαν», «πνigόταν», «μακriού», «καλόκαird» ×2, «ψυχraίνε», «φσιδoλογούσε», «κασκόl» ×2, «οποioσδήποτε», «essa»…); invisible zero-width characters [1269] [1271] [1469].
- **Reversed meanings:** blurb made Sam the debtor [34] («Ένας σιδηρουργός χρωστούσε είκοσι δολάρια» — he is owed); "That's not nothing" → «Αυτό δεν είναι τίποτα» [506]; Martha "no one's fool" → she fooled nobody [387]; "I said" given to Nick [280]; Jessica "I'm turning down a rescue" → «Απορρίπτετε» [1471]; "Theo had the theft" → «έκανε την κλοπή» [1399] [1403].
- **Mistranslations ×77**, e.g. holly = «γκι» (mistletoe) ×6; bulbs = «βόλοι» (marbles); "the green" as «το πράσινο» (the colour) [272]; errand = «θεώρημα» (theorem) [1365]; crate = «ντουλάπα»; cottage = «καλύβα»; misfiled = «παραπέμφθηκαν»; snowplough = «οργώνει τους δρόμους»; trudged = «κουτσαίνοντας»; wobbly chairs = «τζογάρουν» (gamble); "the woman he was bleeding" = «η γυναίκα που αιμορραγούσε»; Society = «Σύλλογος» [1323]; boot camp / bunting left in English.
- **Untranslated English (ruling 3):** banner FROST FALLS THROUGH THE YEARS [303], drawer labels DEEDS/PERMITS/TAX ROLLS/PROPERTY RECORDS [724], file tab PROPERTY — EVERGREEN AVE [1253], "Mrs./Mr." titles, Evergreen Avenue / Maple Street ×5, boot camp ×2, «Μπάντινγκ»; 320 Latin-script names (FP-EL-5).
- **Gender:** Pip masculine ×5 ([1292] [1528] [1548], «ο οποίος» [782]); Fiona «Ο μόνος» [179]; Vera «ο άλλος» [760]; Jessica «ένας από εμάς / Ένας ξένος» [1509]; the circle women «γέροι/άχρηστοι» [387], «οι δυο μας» masculine [385] [101]; Fiona «Τον κοίταξα» [1176]; Prancer masculine [867] (EN "she").
- **Address (ruling 2):** Tanaka informal to "Mrs. Claus" in Ch21; Sam, Jamal, Vera, Neil addressed informally by Jessica; Bram formal to Jessica; Greta formal — all aligned (table below).
- **Units:** four acres → «τέσσερα στρέμματα» (a quarter of the real area) → «σχεδόν δεκαέξι στρέμματα»; six feet → δύο μέτρα; inch/quarter-inch/yards → πόντοι/μέτρα; figurative inches → πόντοι. "Fifty miles" kept (ruling 4). Currency: dollars only.
- **Cider (ruling 6):** «μηλίτης» (alcoholic) ×11 → «(ζεστός) χυμός μήλου».
- **Stray `<bt>` (ruling 5):** 8 removed; title anchors added on the title page and series list as in Books 1/3.
- **Excerpt:** the MT excerpt replaced by Flour Play Ch1 from the corrected Flour Play Greek (fixes e.g. «Πολύ κοντά» for "Very nearly" → «Σχεδόν», «Απόκριες» for Halloween, 15 cm for six inches, Χιονούλης); 8 paragraphs adapted to Inn Trouble's older-draft English (IT-EL-8).
- **Structure:** 3 stray heading lines «Κεφάλαιο 3» inside Ch4 [321], Ch7 [498], Ch14 [838]; all 31 chapter texts present and complete; the +81 paragraph gap is the Greek splitting dialogue from narration (Ch9 +25, Ch16 +15, Ch26 +12, Ch18 +11, Ch31 +11, Ch14 +10) plus back-matter `***`; no omission or duplicate text found (IT-EL-1). Narrative tense αόριστος + παρατατικός throughout (present slips fixed).

## Address table used (ruling 2)
- Informal both ways: circle women; Nick; Fiona; Bram (ruling; MT had him formal — fixed); Greta (as Book 3); Mark; children ← Jessica; Theo ↔ Jessica (first names in the English, friends; IT-EL-5 — differs from Book 3's formal Theo); Noriko ↔ Jessica from Ch25 ("Jessica"/"Noriko" in the English).
- Formal both ways: Sam Patel (MT had Jessica informal Ch8/Ch17 — fixed), Jamal, Vera (Ch11/23/26/27 fixed), Neil Carter, the mayor, the Worthingtons, Mrs. Pelletier; Noriko ↔ Jessica in Ch6 and Ch21 (Ch21 MT informal — fixed); Noriko ↔ Theo/Vera (professional). Emma → Jessica formal («κυρία Κλος, κοιτάξτε»).
